<script>
const list = document.getElementById("tickets");
new EventSource("/live").onmessage = (e) => {
  const li = document.createElement("li");
  li.className = "new";
  li.textContent = JSON.parse(e.data).title;
  list.prepend(li);
};
document.getElementById("add").onsubmit = (e) => {
  e.preventDefault();
  const title = e.target.elements.title.value;
  e.target.reset();
  fetch("/tickets", { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ title }) });
};
</script>

new EventSource("/live").onmessage = (e) => {
  const li = document.createElement("li");
  li.className = "new";
  li.textContent = JSON.parse(e.data).title;
  list.prepend(li);
};
document.getElementById("add").onsubmit = (e) => {
  e.preventDefault();
  const title = e.target.elements.title.value;
  e.target.reset();
  fetch("/tickets", { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ title }) });
};
</script>

<ul id="ticks"></ul>
<script>
const list = document.getElementById("ticks");
new EventSource("/live").onmessage = (e) => {
  const tick = JSON.parse(e.data);
  const li = document.createElement("li");
  li.className = "new";
  li.textContent = "tick " + tick.n + " at " + new Date(tick.at * 1000).toLocaleTimeString();
  list.prepend(li);
};
</script>
